Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the systematics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 462 and the convergence rate is O(174/n^5).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by systematics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 462, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 174. QED.
